| safeloadai trust-inert-from | 902 / 902 | 742 / 3801 | - | 51 | 0 | 0 | 0.997 | 2.6292 |
| safeloadai upper bound | 899 / 902 | 263 / 3801 | - | 51 | 0 | 0 | 0.6572 | 2.5933 |
| picklescan | 750 / 902 | 29 / 3801 | - | 389 | 0 | 0 | 0.1212 | 0.249 |
| fickling | 737 / 902 | 1481 / 3801 | - | 2496 | 0 | 0 | 0.0836 | 0.21 |

A detailed per-format comparison is withheld until coordinated disclosure with the affected projects is complete.

"of which UNVERIFIABLE": false positives whose every reason is UNVERIFIABLE, so a user could accept them, item by item, for that one file. They still count as false positives. Malicious files that are UNVERIFIABLE only: 3 (an override would let these through, which is why it names every item).

## Per group

Groups: `lab` and `mutations` are generated marker payloads; `hf-malicious` are Hub files the Hub's scanners flag, labelled from that evidence; `hf-tune` and `hf-heldout` are the two halves of the benign Hub corpus, split by repo. The allowlist review looked only at `hf-tune`; the headline false-positive rate is `hf-heldout`. FP MALICIOUS / RISKY / UNVERIFIABLE split safeloadai's false positives by category.
